   4368  f3c3				   boxcollision
   4369  f3c3				  -	      ifconst	BOXCOLLISION
   4370  f3c3				  -			; the worst case cycle-time for the code below is 43 cycles.
   4371  f3c3				  -			; unfortunately, prior to getting here we've burned 44 cycles in argument setup. eep!
   4372  f3c3				  -
   4373  f3c3				  -			;__boxx1 = accumulator
   4374  f3c3				  -			;__boxy1 = y
   4375  f3c3				  -__boxw1    =	temp3
   4376  f3c3				  -__boxh1    =	temp4
   4377  f3c3				  -
   4378  f3c3				  -__boxx2    =	temp5
   4379  f3c3				  -__boxy2    =	temp6
   4380  f3c3				  -__boxw2    =	temp7
   4381  f3c3				  -__boxh2    =	temp8
   4382  f3c3				  -
   4383  f3c3				  -DoXCollisionCheck
   4384  f3c3				  -			;lda __boxx1 ; skipped. already in the accumulator
   4385  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxx2	;3
   4386  f3c3				  -	      bcs	X1isbiggerthanX2	;2/3
   4387  f3c3				  -X2isbiggerthanX1
   4388  f3c3				  -			; carry is clear
   4389  f3c3				  -	      adc	__boxw1	;3
   4390  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxx2	;3
   4391  f3c3				  -	      bcs	DoYCollisionCheck	;3/2
   4392  f3c3				  -	      rts		;6 - carry clear, no collision
   4393  f3c3				  -X1isbiggerthanX2
   4394  f3c3				  -	      clc		;2
   4395  f3c3				  -	      sbc	__boxw2	;3
   4396  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxx2	;3
   4397  f3c3				  -	      bcs	noboxcollision	;3/2
   4398  f3c3				  -DoYCollisionCheck
   4399  f3c3				  -	      tya		; 2 ; use to be "lda __boxy1"
   4400  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxy2	;3
   4401  f3c3				  -	      bcs	Y1isbiggerthanY2	;3/2
   4402  f3c3				  -Y2isbiggerthanY1
   4403  f3c3				  -			; carry is clear
   4404  f3c3				  -	      adc	__boxh1	;3
   4405  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxy2	;3
   4406  f3c3				  -	      rts		;6
   4407  f3c3				  -Y1isbiggerthanY2
   4408  f3c3				  -	      clc		;2
   4409  f3c3				  -	      sbc	__boxh2	;3
   4410  f3c3				  -	      cmp	__boxy2	;3
   4411  f3c3				  -	      bcs	noboxcollision	;3/2
   4412  f3c3				  -yesboxcollision
   4413  f3c3				  -	      sec		;2
   4414  f3c3				  -	      rts		;6
   4415  f3c3				  -noboxcollision
   4416  f3c3				  -	      clc		;2
   4417  f3c3				  -	      rts		;6
   4418  f3c3					      endif		; BOXCOLLISION
   4419  f3c3
   4420  f3c3				   randomize
   4421  f3c3		       a5 40		      lda	rand
   4422  f3c5		       4a		      lsr
   4423  f3c6		       26 41		      rol	rand16
   4424  f3c8		       90 02		      bcc	noeor
   4425  f3ca		       49 b4		      eor	#$B4
   4426  f3cc				   noeor
   4427  f3cc		       85 40		      sta	rand
   4428  f3ce		       45 41		      eor	rand16
   4429  f3d0		       60		      rts
   4430  f3d1
   4431  f3d1							; *** bcd conversion routine courtesy Omegamatrix
   4432  f3d1							; *** http://atariage.com/forums/blog/563/entry-10832-hex-to-bcd-conversion-0-99/
   4433  f3d1				  -	      ifconst	.calledfunction_converttobcd
   4434  f3d1				  -converttobcd
   4435  f3d1				  -			;value to convert is in the accumulator
   4436  f3d1				  -	      sta	temp1
   4437  f3d1				  -	      lsr
   4438  f3d1				  -	      adc	temp1
   4439  f3d1				  -	      ror
   4440  f3d1				  -	      lsr
   4441  f3d1				  -	      lsr
   4442  f3d1				  -	      adc	temp1
   4443  f3d1				  -	      ror
   4444  f3d1				  -	      adc	temp1
   4445  f3d1				  -	      ror
   4446  f3d1				  -	      lsr
   4447  f3d1				  -	      and	#$3C
   4448  f3d1				  -	      sta	temp2
   4449  f3d1				  -	      lsr
   4450  f3d1				  -	      adc	temp2
   4451  f3d1				  -	      adc	temp1
   4452  f3d1				  -	      rts		; return the result in the accumulator
   4453  f3d1					      endif		; .calledfunction_converttobcd

   5198  f47a				  -			; The below code amounts to mousecodey0=mousecodey0-(mousecodey0/8)
   5199  f47a				  -			; +ve mousecodey0 is converted to -ve to do the calculation, and then
   5200  f47a				  -			; negated again because truncation during BCD math results in
   5201  f47a				  -			; differing magnitudes, depending if the value is +ve or -ve.

   5260  f47a				  -			; the actual position. This actual position is stored in mousey1

   5294  f47a				  -			; 0000YyXx st mouse

   5757  f47a				   paddlebuttonhandler		; outside of conditional, for button-handler LUT
   5758  f47a				  -	      ifconst	PADDLESUPPORT
   5759  f47a				  -			; x=0|1 for port, rather than paddle #.
   5760  f47a				  -			; Only the first paddle button will integrate into "joy0fire" testing. If the
   5761  f47a				  -			; game wants to support 2 paddles, up to the game to instead test the
   5762  f47a				  -			; joystick right+left directions instead.
   5763  f47a				  -	      lda	SWCHA	; top of nibble is first paddle button
   5764  f47a				  -	      cpx	#0	; port 0?
   5765  f47a				  -	      beq	skippaddleport2shift
   5766  f47a				  -	      asl		; shift second port to upper nibble
   5767  f47a				  -	      asl
   5768  f47a				  -	      asl
   5769  f47a				  -	      asl
   5770  f47a				  -skippaddleport2shift
   5771  f47a				  -	      and	#%11000000
   5772  f47a				  -	      eor	#%11000000	; invert
   5773  f47a				  -	      sta	sINPT1,x
   5774  f47a				  -	      jmp	buttonreadloopreturn
   5775  f47a					      endif		; PADDLESUPPORT
   5776  f47a
   5777  f47a				   mousebuttonhandler		; outside of conditional, for button-handler LUT
   5778  f47a				  -	      ifconst	MOUSESUPPORT
   5779  f47a				  -			; stick the mouse buttons in the correct shadow register...
   5780  f47a				  -	      txa
   5781  f47a				  -	      asl
   5782  f47a				  -	      tay		; y=x*2
   5783  f47a				  -	      lda	INPT4,x
   5784  f47a				  -	      eor	#%10000000
   5785  f47a				  -	      lsr
   5786  f47a				  -	      sta	sINPT1,x
   5787  f47a				  -
   5788  f47a				  -	      lda	INPT1,y
   5789  f47a				  -	      and	#%10000000
   5790  f47a				  -	      eor	#%10000000
   5791  f47a				  -	      ora	sINPT1,x
   5792  f47a				  -	      sta	sINPT1,x
   5793  f47a				  -	      jmp	buttonreadloopreturn
   5794  f47a					      endif		; MOUSESUPPORT
   5795  f47a
   5796  f47a				  -	      ifconst	KEYPADSUPPORT
   5797  f47a				  -			; ** select keypad rows 0 to 3 over 4 frames...
   5798  f47a				  -keypadrowselect
   5799  f47a				  -	      inc	keypadcounter
   5800  f47a				  -	      ldy	#0
   5801  f47a				  -	      lda	port0control
   5802  f47a				  -	      cmp	#7
   5803  f47a				  -	      bne	skipport0val
   5804  f47a				  -	      iny		; y=y+1
   5805  f47a				  -skipport0val
   5806  f47a				  -	      lda	port1control
   5807  f47a				  -	      cmp	#7
   5808  f47a				  -	      bne	skipport1val
   5809  f47a				  -	      iny
   5810  f47a				  -	      iny		; y=y+2
   5811  f47a				  -skipport1val
   5812  f47a				  -	      cpy	#0
   5813  f47a				  -	      beq	exitkeypadrowselect
   5814  f47a				  -	      lda	keyrowdirectionmask,y
   5815  f47a				  -	      sta	CTLSWA
   5816  f47a				  -	      tya
   5817  f47a				  -	      asl
   5818  f47a				  -	      asl
   5819  f47a				  -	      sta	inttemp1
   5820  f47a				  -	      lda	keypadcounter
   5821  f47a				  -	      and	#3
   5822  f47a				  -	      ora	inttemp1
   5823  f47a				  -	      tax
   5824  f47a				  -	      lda	keyrowselectvalue,x
   5825  f47a				  -	      sta	SWCHA
   5826  f47a				  -exitkeypadrowselect
   5827  f47a				  -	      rts
   5828  f47a				  -
   5829  f47a				  -keyrowdirectionmask
   5830  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%00000000	; 0 : port0=input port1=input
   5831  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%11110000	; 1 : port0=output port1=input
   5832  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%00001111	; 2 : port0=input port1=output
   5833  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%11111111	; 3 : port0=output port1=output
   5834  f47a				  -
   5835  f47a				  -keyrowselectvalue
   5836  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%00000000, #%00000000, #%00000000, #%00000000	; no row selected, all pins high, always
   5837  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%11100000, #%11010000, #%10110000, #%01110000	; p0 keypad in
   5838  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%00001110, #%00001101, #%00001011, #%00000111	; p1 keypad in
   5839  f47a				  -	      .byte	#%11101110, #%11011101, #%10111011, #%01110111	; p0+p1 keypads in
   5840  f47a					      endif		; KEYPADSUPPORT

   5881  f47a				  -	      lda	INPT3
   5882  f47a				  -	      cmp	#$80
   5883  f47a				  -	      rol	keypadmatrix1a,x
   5884  f47a				  -	      lda	INPT5
   5885  f47a				  -	      cmp	#$80
   5886  f47a				  -	      rol	keypadmatrix1a,x

   5899  f484
   5900  f484				   setonebuttonmode
   5901  f484		       a9 06		      lda	#6	; in case we're in unlocked-bios mode
   5902  f486		       85 01		      sta	VBLANK	; if we were on paddles, the line is grounded out.
   5903  f488		       a9 14		      lda	#$14
   5904  f48a		       8d 83 02 	      sta	CTLSWB
   5905  f48d		       ad 82 02 	      lda	SWCHB
   5906  f490		       1d aa f4 	      ora	thisjoy2buttonbit,x	; disable: write 1 to the 2-button bit
   5907  f493		       8d 82 02 	      sta	SWCHB
   5908  f496		       60		      rts
   5909  f497
   5910  f497				   settwobuttonmode
   5911  f497		       a9 06		      lda	#6	; in case we're in unlocked-bios mode
   5912  f499		       85 01		      sta	VBLANK	; if we were on paddles, the line is grounded out.
   5913  f49b		       a9 14		      lda	#$14
   5914  f49d		       8d 83 02 	      sta	CTLSWB
   5915  f4a0		       ad 82 02 	      lda	SWCHB
   5916  f4a3		       3d ab f4 	      and	thisjoy2buttonbit+1,x	; enable: write 0 to the 2-button bit
   5917  f4a6		       8d 82 02 	      sta	SWCHB
   5918  f4a9		       60		      rts

   5970  f4ad
   5971  f4ad							;******** more or less the Atari recommended startup procedure
   5972  f4ad
   5973  f4ad		       78		      sei
   5974  f4ae		       d8		      cld
   5975  f4af
   5976  f4af					      ifnconst	NOTIALOCK
   5977  f4af		       a9 07		      lda	#$07
   5978  f4b1				  -	      else
   5979  f4b1				  -	      lda	#$06
   5980  f4b1					      endif
   5981  f4b1		       85 01		      sta	INPTCTRL	;lock 7800 into 7800 mode
   5982  f4b3		       a9 7f		      lda	#$7F
   5983  f4b5		       85 3c		      sta	CTRL	;disable DMA
   5984  f4b7		       a9 00		      lda	#$00
   5985  f4b9		       85 38		      sta	OFFSET
   5986  f4bb					      ifnconst	NOTIALOCK
   5987  f4bb		       85 01		      sta	INPTCTRL
   5988  f4bd		       85 20		      sta	BACKGRND	; black default, in case a flash cart is using something else
   5989  f4bf					      endif
   5990  f4bf		       a2 ff		      ldx	#$FF
   5991  f4c1		       9a		      txs
   5992  f4c2
   5993  f4c2							;************** Clear Memory
   5994  f4c2
   5995  f4c2							; ** Clear 1800-27FF, pg0+pg1 memory.
   5996  f4c2				   ClearMemPages
   5997  f4c2		       a9 00		      lda	#0
   5998  f4c4		       a8		      tay		; y=0
   5999  f4c5		       85 80		      sta	$80
   6000  f4c7		       a2 18		      ldx	#$18
   6001  f4c9				   ClearMemPagesLoop
   6002  f4c9		       86 81		      stx	$81	; needed for when we step on ZP memory
   6003  f4cb		       91 80		      sta	($80),y	;Store data
   6004  f4cd		       c8		      iny		;Next byte
   6005  f4ce		       d0 f9		      bne	ClearMemPagesLoop
   6006  f4d0		       e8		      inx
   6007  f4d1		       e0 28		      cpx	#$28
   6008  f4d3		       d0 f4		      bne	ClearMemPagesLoop
   6009  f4d5		       85 81		      sta	$81
   6010  f4d7
   6011  f4d7							;seed random number with hopefully-random timer value
   6012  f4d7		       a9 01		      lda	#1
   6013  f4d9		       0d 84 02 	      ora	INTIM
   6014  f4dc		       85 40		      sta	rand
   6015  f4de
   6016  f4de							; detect the console type...
   6017  f4de				   pndetectvblankstart
   6018  f4de		       a5 28		      lda	MSTAT
   6019  f4e0		       10 fc		      bpl	pndetectvblankstart	; if we're not in VBLANK, wait for it to start
   6020  f4e2				   pndetectvblankover
   6021  f4e2		       a5 28		      lda	MSTAT
   6022  f4e4		       30 fc		      bmi	pndetectvblankover	; then wait for it to be over
   6023  f4e6		       a0 00		      ldy	#$00
   6024  f4e8		       a2 00		      ldx	#$00
   6025  f4ea				   pndetectvblankhappening
   6026  f4ea		       a5 28		      lda	MSTAT
   6027  f4ec		       30 07		      bmi	pndetectinvblank	; if VBLANK starts, exit our counting loop
   6028  f4ee		       85 24		      sta	WSYNC

   6438  fff8				  -	      ORG	$87FF8
   6439  fff8				  -	      RORG	$FFF8
   6440  fff8				  -	      endif
   6441  fff8					      endif
   6442  fff8
   6443  fff8
   6444  fff8		       ff		      .byte.b	$FF	; region verification. $FF=all regions
   6445  fff9		       f7		      .byte.b	$F7	; high nibble:  encryption check from $N000 to $FF7F. we only hash the last 4k for faster boot.
   6446  fffa							; low nibble :  N=7 atari rainbow start, N=3 no atari rainbow
   6447  fffa
